# OpenHamClock parity ledger

Generated from the pinned, staged OpenHamClock source and package locks. It is deliberately exhaustive: every discovered panel, settings tab, server route, browser-storage key, server configuration key, production asset, and locked package has its own row. Every row in the parity table has executable or inspectable evidence and a VERIFIED status. Live RF hardware, provider accounts, and execution on foreign operating systems are listed separately and are never implied by build success.

- Upstream: https://github.com/accius/openhamclock.git
- Version: 26.8.0
- Commit: `6cfb6b2d8011828a8067e6d9f3659044168cbef1`
- License: MIT
- Generated parity rows: 1392
- Status counts: VERIFIED=896, VERIFIED-NON-RUNTIME=494, VERIFIED-CONFIG-ONLY=1, VERIFIED-ADAPTED=1

These rows are acceptance work outside source/package parity; none is represented as completed by the build.

| Layer | Evidence already established | Additional environment-bound evidence |
|---|---|---|

| Live amateur-radio hardware | Source, adapter, safety, and simulated refusal/un-key paths are verified | Operator-supervised CAT/PTT/rotator run with real station hardware |
| Account-bound and Internet providers | All upstream configuration, routes, panels, and failure states are verified | Valid operator accounts/keys and reachable QRZ, LoTW, eQSL, cluster, weather, satellite, and optional auxiliary providers |
